वरील ठिकाणी सध्या काम चालू आहे त् याचे साराांश खालील प्र माणे आहेत.
 प्रस्
तावना
नगर पठरषद /पांचायत/महानगरपाललका क्षेत्र ातील नागठरकाांना स् वच्छ, लनरोगी आलण प्र दूषणमुक्त वातावरण
उपलब्ध करून देण्यासािी घनकचरा सांकलन व वाहतूक व्य वस्था प्र भावीपणे राबलवणे आवश्यक आहे. या
कामामध्ये घरगुती , व्य ावसालयक तसेच साववजलनक ठिकाणाांवरील कचऱ्याचे लनयलमत सांकलन , वगीकरण आलण
वाहतूक याांचा समावेश होतो.
कामाचे स् वरूप
1. घराघरातून ओला व सुका कचरा स् वतांत्रपणे सांकललत करणे.
2. बाजारपेि, रस्ते , साववजलनक ठिकाणे, शाळा, कायावलये इत्यादी भागाांतील कचरा गोळा करणे.
3. सांकललत कचऱ्याची लनधावठरत ट्र ान्सफर स् टेशन ककांवा प्रक्र िया केंद्रापयंत वाहतूक करणे.
4. घांटागाडी, ठटप्पर , ट्रॅक् टर , कॉम्पॅक्टर वाहनाांचा वापर करून दररोज कचरा उचल करणे.
5. कचरा सांकलन करताना स् वच्छता , सुरलक्षतता व पयाववरणीय लनयमाांचे पालन करणे.
6. नागठरकाांमध्ये स् वच्छता व कचरा वगीकरणाबाबत जनजागृती करणे.

सदर काम प्रर् ािीपणे , तनयमांनुसार ि पयाािरणपूरक पद्धिीने पार पाडण्यासाठी आिश्यक मनुष्यबळ ,
यंत्रसामग्री , िाहने ि िांतत्रक सुतिधा उपलब्ध करून देण्याि येिील. िसेच ओला ि सुका कचरा स् ििंत्रपणे
संकतलि करून त् याचे योग्य व्य िस्थापन करण्याि येईल. नालयांमधील गाळ तनयतमिपणे काढून तनधााररि

घनकचरा व्य िस्थापन कृिी आराखडा
जालना महानगर पातलका , जालना
1. प्रस् िािना
हा घनकचरा व्य िस्थापन (Solid Waste Management) कृिी आराखडा जालना महानगरपातलकेसाठी
ियार करण्याि आलेला असून िो तनतिदेमधील कामाच्या	 व्य	 ाप्तीनुसार ियार करण्याि आला आहे. या
आराखड्यामध्ये घरगुिी, व्य ािसातयक , हॉटेल, रेस्टॉरंट िसेच सािाजतनक रठकाणांमधून कचरा संकलन, ओला
ि सुका कचरा िगीकरण, िाहिूक, प्रक्र िया ि िैज्ञातनक पद्धिीने तिलहेिाट लािणे यांचा समािेश आहे.
या योजनेचा मुख्य उद्देश शहर स् िच्छिा , पयाािरण संरक्षण आतण सािाजतनक आरोग्य सुधारणा हा आहे.
2. प्र कलपाची उक्रद्दष्टे
1. शहरामध्ये 100% घरगुिी कचरा संकलन व्य िस्था राबतिणे.
2. ओला ि सुका कचरा िेगिेगळा संकतलि करणे.
3. कचऱ्याची तनयतमि िाहिूक ि िैज्ञातनक	 प्रक्र	 िया करणे.
4. नाले ि गटारीमधील गाळ तनयतमि काढणे.
5. कचरा डेपोिरील र् ार कमी करून जास्िीि जास्ि कचरा प्रक्र िया करणे.
6. शहर स् िच्छिा ि सािाजतनक आरोग्य सुधारणे.
7.	 स्	 िच्छ	 र्	 ारि अतर्यानाच्या उक्रद्दष्टांची पूिािा करणे.
3. कामाची व्य ाप्ती
3.1 घर िे घर कचरा संकलन
 घरगुिी, दुकाने, हॉटेल, रेस्टॉरंट , बाजारपेठ, संस्था ि सािाजतनक रठकाणांमधून कचरा संकलन.
 दररोज ऑटो रटपर, ई-ररक्षा , हािगाडी ि कॉम्पॅक्टरच्या माध्यमािून कचरा उचल.
 ओला ि सुका कचरा स् ििंत्रपणे संकतलि करणे.
 िाडातनहाय तनतिि िेळापत्रकानुसार कचरा संकलन.

TECHNICAL PROPOSAL
For Municipal Solid Waste Management Services
Submitted By:
Berojgaranchi Kshitij Nagari Seva Sahakari Sanstha, Amravati (BKNSSS)
Submitted To: Jalana Municipal Corporation.
Name of Work: Collecting waste from households, hotels, restaurants etc. in Jalna city,
segregating it into wet and dry, dumping it at the waste collection center, and transporting the
waste from the waste collection center to Samangaon, a solid waste processing
Tender No.: 2026_DMA_1312294_2
1. INTRODUCTION
Berojgaranchi Kshitij Nagari Seva Sahakari Sanstha, Amravati (BKNSSS) hereby submits its
Technical Proposal for implementation of the Municipal Solid Waste Management project.
The proposed system is designed to establish an efficient, accountable and technology-enabled
mechanism for collection, segregation, transportation, monitoring and handling of municipal
solid waste.
The operational approach will focus on regular door-to-door collection, source segregation,
optimized vehicle deployment, GPS-based fleet monitoring, QR-based collection monitoring,
and proper operation of MRF and RTS facilities wherever included in the scope, effective
complaint redressal and continuous IEC & citizen engagement.
A. UNDERSTANDING OF PROJECT
The Agency understands that effective Solid Waste Management is not limited to collection and
transportation of waste. It requires an integrated system covering the entire operational chain
from the waste generator to the designated processing/disposal facility.
The proposed project will therefore be implemented with emphasis on:
1. Door-to-door collection of municipal solid waste from households, commercial
establishments, shops, hotels, institutions, markets and other designated waste generators.

-- 619 of 717 --

मुख्य कार्ाालर् : प्र भात कॉलनी, शिलाांगन रोड, गजानन महाराज मांशिर जवळ, अमरावती
मो.९०२२५०३५७०, ९९२११७१५५५ Mail id : dineshpgahalod@gmail.com
2. Collection of segregated wet and dry waste and prevention of unnecessary mixing during
collection and transportation.
3. Ward-wise and route-wise deployment of collection vehicles.
4. Timely transportation of collected waste to designated MRF, RTS, processing or disposal
facilities.
5. Deployment and supervision of adequate manpower.
6. GPS-based tracking and monitoring of collection vehicles.
7. QR-based monitoring of household/establishment waste collection wherever applicable.
8. Operation and monitoring of Material Recovery Facility (MRF).
9. Operation and management of Refuse Transfer Station (RTS), wherever applicable.
10. Establishment of a systematic citizen complaint redressal mechanism.
11. IEC activities for source segregation, cleanliness and responsible waste disposal.
12. Daily documentation, MIS reporting and performance monitoring.
The objective shall be to create a reliable, traceable, measurable and citizen-oriented Solid Waste
Management system with clear responsibility at each operational level.
B. DEPLOYMENT METHODOLOGY
Before commencement of full-scale operations, BKNSSS shall undertake a detailed field
assessment of the project area.
The deployment shall broadly be undertaken in the following stages:
Stage 1 – Baseline Assessment: Survey of wards, residential areas, commercial establishments,
markets, bulk waste generators, existing collection points, MRF/RTS facilities,
processing/disposal locations and existing collection arrangements.
Stage 2 – Route Planning: The entire project area shall be divided into practical collection routes.
Routes shall be planned considering population, number of properties, road accessibility,
expected waste quantity, distance, vehicle capacity and travel time.
Stage 3 – Resource Mapping: Vehicles, drivers, helpers, sanitation workers, supervisors and
technical personnel shall be mapped against each route/ward.
Stage 4 – Trial Run and Route Optimization: Initial route operations shall be monitored and
routes shall be modified wherever required to reduce missed properties, unnecessary vehicle
movement and delays.
Stage 5 – Full Deployment: Regular collection and transportation operations shall commence
according to approved schedules.

-- 620 of 717 --

मुख्य कार्ाालर् : प्र भात कॉलनी, शिलाांगन रोड, गजानन महाराज मांशिर जवळ, अमरावती
मो.९०२२५०३५७०, ९९२११७१५५५ Mail id : dineshpgahalod@gmail.com
Stage 6 – Daily Monitoring: Supervisors shall monitor attendance, route commencement, vehicle
movement, collection coverage, segregation, trips, complaints and completion of assigned routes.
Stage 7 – Reporting & Corrective Action: Deviations such as missed collection, vehicle
breakdown, absenteeism, route delay or citizen complaints shall be recorded and corrective
action initiated.
C. FLEET DEPLOYMENT PLAN
Vehicles shall be deployed ward-wise and route-wise according to the actual requirement and
tender conditions.
The proposed fleet deployment register shall contain:
Vehicle Type Primary Purpose Deployment
Door-to-Door Collection Vehicle Primary waste collection Ward/Route-wise
Refuse Compactor Transportation/compaction Designated routes
Tipper/Other Vehicle Secondary transportation As required
MRF/RTS Supporting Vehicle Internal/secondary movement Facility-wise
Each vehicle shall preferably be assigned a unique identification/route number. Daily
deployment records shall capture driver/helper details, starting time, assigned route, trips
completed and closing status.
Preventive maintenance shall be planned so that avoidable breakdowns do not disrupt collection.
In case of breakdown, alternate deployment shall be arranged as per contractual requirements
and available resources.
GPS data and field supervision shall be used to verify route completion and vehicle utilization.
D. MANPOWER DEPLOYMENT PLAN
Manpower shall be deployed according to workload, number of routes, vehicles, wards and
facilities covered under the contract.
The proposed structure shall include:

E. GPS MONITORING SYSTEM
A GPS-based monitoring mechanism shall be used for effective fleet supervision wherever

The GPS monitoring system shall facilitate monitoring of:

 Route followed by the vehicle;
 Start and end time of operation;
 Trip history;
 Stoppage/idle time;
 Route deviation;
 Vehicle utilization; and
 Completion of assigned collection routes.
A centralized monitoring mechanism shall be maintained for supervisors/authorized municipal
officials as required.

मुख्य कार्ाालर् : प्र भात कॉलनी, शिलाांगन रोड, गजानन महाराज मांशिर जवळ, अमरावती

GPS reports shall be used along with field verification and operational registers for performance
monitoring.
In case of route deviation, prolonged stoppage or missed route, the concerned supervisor/driver
shall be contacted and corrective action shall be taken.
F. QR-BASED COLLECTION MONITORING
SYSTEM
Where QR-based monitoring is required, properties/households/establishments shall be mapped
with unique QR identification as per the approved system.
During collection, authorized collection personnel shall scan the relevant QR code through the
designated application/device.
The system may capture operational information such as:
Property ID → Date → Collection Time → Collection Status → Route/Vehicle →
User/Collector
The monitoring dashboard shall assist in identifying collected and missed properties and in
reviewing ward-wise/route-wise collection coverage.
Missed properties shall be communicated to the field supervisor for verification and corrective
collection wherever required.
The QR-based system will therefore support traceability, accountability and evidence-based
monitoring of door-to-door collection.
G. MRF OPERATION PLAN
Where Material Recovery Facility (MRF) operation forms part of the tender scope, segregated
dry waste received at the facility shall be managed systematically.
The broad workflow shall be:
Receipt of Dry Waste → Weighing/Recording → Sorting → Category-wise Segregation →
Temporary Storage → Recovery/Dispatch to Authorized Channel

-- 623 of 717 --

मुख्य कार्ाालर् : प्र भात कॉलनी, शिलाांगन रोड, गजानन महाराज मांशिर जवळ, अमरावती
मो.९०२२५०३५७०, ९९२११७१५५५ Mail id : dineshpgahalod@gmail.com
Dry recyclable material may be separated into applicable categories such as paper/cardboard,
plastic, metal, glass and other recoverable fractions according to the facility arrangement and
contractual requirements.
Records shall be maintained for incoming waste, recovered material, rejects and outgoing
material.
MRF operations shall emphasize housekeeping, worker safety, fire prevention, PPE usage and
proper storage.
No waste/material shall be disposed of outside the approved system.
H. RTS OPERATION PLAN
Where a Refuse Transfer Station (RTS) is included in the scope, the facility shall function as an
organized intermediate point between primary collection and onward transportation.
Collection vehicles arriving at the RTS shall be recorded and waste shall be transferred to the
designated larger transport system/equipment in accordance with the approved operating
procedure.
The basic operational sequence shall be:
Arrival of Collection Vehicle → Entry/Recording → Unloading/Transfer → Loading into
Transport Vehicle → Dispatch to Designated Facility → Cleaning of Transfer Area
The Agency shall aim to minimize unnecessary accumulation of waste at the RTS.
Daily records of incoming vehicles/trips, outgoing trips, quantity where measurable, cleaning
and operational issues shall be maintained.
I. COMPLAINT REDRESSAL SYSTEM
A structured complaint management mechanism shall be established to receive, record, assign,
monitor and close citizen complaints.
Complaints may relate to missed collection, delayed collection, overflowing waste points,
improper collection, vehicle issues or other services included under the contract.

-- 624 of 717 --

मुख्य कार्ाालर् : प्र भात कॉलनी, शिलाांगन रोड, गजानन महाराज मांशिर जवळ, अमरावती
मो.९०२२५०३५७०, ९९२११७१५५५ Mail id : dineshpgahalod@gmail.com
The proposed workflow shall be:
Complaint Received → Unique Complaint Entry → Ward/Location Identification →
Assignment to Concerned Supervisor → Field Action → Verification → Closure
A complaint register/MIS shall record the complaint date and time, complainant/location details
as applicable, nature of complaint, responsible team, action taken and closure status.
Pending complaints shall be reviewed periodically by the Project Manager/authorized supervisor.
Recurring complaints shall be analyzed to identify systemic operational issues.
J. IEC AND CITIZEN ENGAGEMENT PLAN
IEC activities shall support operational SWM activities by encouraging citizens to participate in
source segregation and proper waste handover.
The IEC programme shall focus on:
 Segregation of wet and dry waste at source;
 Regular handover of waste to authorized collection vehicles;
 Avoidance of littering and open dumping;
 Reduction of single-use plastic wherever applicable;
 Awareness regarding collection timings;
 Responsible waste management by shops, markets and commercial establishments;
 Awareness among bulk waste generators; and
 Citizen participation in cleanliness activities.
Communication activities may include door-to-door awareness, ward-level campaigns, meetings,
posters/banners, public announcements, demonstrations and digital communication, subject to
tender requirements and approval of the authority.
Field teams shall coordinate with municipal officials, community representatives, commercial
associations and other stakeholders.

मुख्य कार्ाालर् : प्र भात कॉलनी, शिलाांगन रोड, गजानन महाराज मांशिर जवळ, अमरावती
मो.९०२२५०३५७०, ९९२११७१५५५ Mail id : dineshpgahalod@gmail.com
MONITORING, MIS AND REPORTING
The Agency shall maintain a systematic monitoring framework integrating field supervision,
GPS monitoring, QR collection data, manpower attendance, vehicle records, MRF/RTS records
and citizen complaints.
A daily/periodic MIS may include collection coverage, vehicles deployed, trips completed,
manpower deployed, missed routes/properties, GPS exceptions, QR collection status, complaints
received/resolved, MRF/RTS operations and significant field issues.
This will enable the competent authority to assess actual field performance and initiate corrective
measures wherever necessary.
CONCLUSION & UNDERTAKING
Berojgaranchi Kshitij Nagari Seva Sahakari Sanstha, Amravati (BKNSSS) undertakes to deploy
the required resources and implement the project in accordance with the approved scope of work,
tender conditions, directions of the competent authority and applicable statutory requirements.
The Agency shall endeavour to maintain continuity of services, effective supervision,
technology-based monitoring, proper documentation and coordination with the concerned
Municipal Authority throughout the contract period.
